# Skills · F · part 10

940 entries, faithful-summary-writer to False. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **faithful-summary-writer**: Writes an accurate summary of one document where every sentence traces to the source and nothing is added. Use when the user says summarize this, TL;DR, executive summary, condense, or shorten without changing facts. Picks length from a ratio table and structure by purpose, then runs a script that flags unsupported sentences, numbers and names missing from the source, altered or flipped figures, dropped hedges, copied passages and the ratio. Do not use for merging several sources, translation, meeting action items, style rewrites, or checking claims against the outside world. ([kobilyricsweb/skillalmanac-skills](https://github.com/kobilyricsweb/skillalmanac-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/faithful-summary-writer))
- **faithful-translation**: This skill should be used when translating an institutional document (a CLAUDE.md, a personal constitution, a set of working principles) into another AI service's format — ChatGPT custom instructions, a Gemini gem, a generic system prompt — or when the user asks "adapt my CLAUDE.md for ChatGPT", "fit my instructions into Gemini", "make a system prompt from my constitution", or is deciding what to cut to meet a length limit. Also consult it when composing or reading the embassy state file. ([saita08/tks08-plugins](https://github.com/saita08/tks08-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/embassy/skills/faithful-translation) · ★1)
- **faithful-translation**: Produces source-faithful translations across any language pair with a parallel sentence ledger for verification, terminology preservation, register matching, and translator's notes ([dobachi/claude-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/dobachi/claude-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/faithful-translation/skills/faithful-translation))
- **faithfulness-check**: Verify that a quoted clinical span actually appears verbatim in the source chart at the claimed location. Use to enforce evidence-grounded answers and reject hallucinated quotes during chart review. ([yuhangjiang22/chart-review-platform](https://github.com/yuhangjiang22/chart-review-platform/tree/HEAD/server/lib/skills/faithfulness-check) · ★4)
- **faithfulness-eval**: Evaluates the faithfulness and causal alignment of chain-of-thought reasoning in LLMs by measuring how much the final answer depends on the generated reasoning steps versus the original question. It uses causal mediation analysis to compute indirect and direct effects, and a simulator-based metric to quantify rationale faithfulness. Use when the user wants to benchmark on StrategyQA, GSM8K, Causal Understanding, Quarel, OpenBookQA, QASC, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Controlled Indirect Effect. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/faithfulness-eval) · ★3)
- **faithfulness-review**: Check every generated profile rule against the source for being stronger than its evidence (over-claim), and write a faithfulness report. v0 compares rules vs raw source text; v1 vs evidence records. ([grammy-jiang/subagent-factory](https://github.com/grammy-jiang/subagent-factory/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/faithfulness-review))
- **faithfulness-scorer**: Score faithfulness of summarization and QA outputs to their source documents, using entailment-based checks, QAGS-style question generation, and fine-grained factuality decomposition. ([HossamBalaha/SkillStack](https://github.com/HossamBalaha/SkillStack/tree/HEAD/ai-output-judgement/faithfulness-scorer))

- **faktencheck**: Prüft einen Text oder eine einzelne Behauptung auf Faktentreue: zieht die prüfbaren Fakten heraus, sucht Belege, unterscheidet widerlegt, nicht belegt und nicht prüfbar und bewertet jede Abweichung danach, wie sehr sie dem Text schadet. Nutze diesen Skill bei „Faktencheck“, „Prüfe diesen Text“, „Stimmt das?“, „Stimmt es, dass …?“, „Ist das korrekt?“ oder wenn ein Text mit der Bitte um Prüfung eingefügt wird. Nicht für die Herkunft eines einzelnen Zitats oder einer Zahl (Herkunftscheck) und nicht für eine Recherche ohne vorliegenden Text (Themenrecherche).
